parser.add_argument(\"--num-workers\", type=int, default=4, help=\"Number of workers\")\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine Test\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Generate corpus\n    print(f\"\\nGenerating {args.num_docs} documents...\")\n    documents = generate_corpus(args.num_docs, seed=42)\n\n    # Generate test queries\n    test_queries = [\n        \"machine learning algorithm\",\n        \"distributed computing system\",\n        \"neural network training\",\n        \"data analysis pipeline\",\n        \"cloud infrastructure deployment\",\n    ] * (args.num_queries // 5)\n\n    print(f\"Number of queries: {len(test_queries)}\")\n    print(f\"Number of workers: {args.num_workers}\")\n\n    # Sequential indexing\n    print(\"\\n--- Sequential Indexing ---\")\n    seq_result = build_tfidf_index_sequential(documents)\n    print(f\"Time: {seq_result.elapsed_time:.3f}s\")\n\n    # Parallel indexing\n    print(\"\\n--- Parallel Indexing ---\")\n    par_result = build_tfidf_index_parallel(documents, num_workers=args.num_workers)\n    print(f\"Time: {par_result.elapsed_time:.3f}s\")\n    print(f\"Speedup: {seq_result.elapsed_time / par_result.elapsed_time:.2f}x\")\n\n    # Verify correctness\n    print(\"\\n--- Verifying Index Correctness ---\")\n    assert par_result.index.num_documents == seq_result.index.num_documents\n    assert par_result.index.vocabulary == seq_result.index.vocabulary\n    assert par_result.index.document_frequencies == seq_result.index.document_frequencies\n    print(\"Index verification passed!\")\n\n    # Sequential search\n    print(\"\\n--- Sequential Batch Search ---\")\n    search_start = time.perf_counter()\n    seq_search_results = batch_search_sequential(test_queries, seq_result.index, top_k=10, documents=documents)\n    seq_search_time = time.perf_counter() - search_start\n    print(f\"Time: {seq_search_time:.3f}s\")\n\n    # Parallel search\n    print(\"\\n--- Parallel Batch Search ---\")\n    par_search_results, par_search_time = batch_search_parallel(\n        test_queries, TF-IDF index building with multiprocessing\n2. Batch similarity search with multiprocessing\n\nAchieves speedup on multi-core systems while maintaining identical results\nto the sequential implementation.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport math\nimport os\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom concurrent.futures import ProcessPoolExecutor\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom heapq import nlargest\nfrom multiprocessing import cpu_count\n\nfrom document_generator import Document\nfrom sequential import (\n    STOP_WORDS,\n    TOKEN_PATTERN,\n    IndexingResult,\n    SearchResult,\n    TFIDFIndex,\n    compute_term_frequencies,\n    tokenize,\n)\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building Result\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Worker Functions for Multiprocessing\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _process_document_chunk(chunk: list[tuple[int, str, str]]) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a chunk of documents to compute term frequencies.\n\n    Args:\n        chunk: List of (doc_id, title, content) tuples\n\n    Returns:\n        Dict with doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, and vocabulary for this chunk\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n    vocabulary = set()\n\n    for doc_id, title, content in chunk:\n        text = title + \" \" + content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        doc_term_freqs[doc_id] = tf\n        doc_terms[doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        vocabulary.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return {\n        'doc_term_freqs': doc_term_freqs,\n        'doc_terms': doc_terms,\n        'vocabulary': vocabulary,\n    }\n\n\ndef _compute_df_for_terms(args: tuple[list[str], dict[int, set[str]]]) -> dict[str, int]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute document frequencies for a subset of terms.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (terms_subset, doc_terms)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dict mapping term -> document frequency\n    \"\"\"\n    terms_subset, doc_terms = args\n    result = {}\n    for term in terms_subset:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        result[term] = df\n    return result\n\n\ndef _build_posting_list_chunk(args: tuple[list[str], dict[int, dict[str, float]], dict[str, float]]) -> dict[str, list[tuple[int, float]]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build posting lists for a chunk of terms.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (terms, doc_term_freqs, idf)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dict mapping term -> posting list\n    \"\"\"\n    terms, doc_term_freqs, idf = args\n    result = {}\n\n    for term in terms:\n        posting_list = []\n        term_idf = idf[term]\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tf = tf_dict[term]\n                tfidf = tf * term_idf\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n        # Sort by score descending\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        result[term] = posting_list\n\n    return result\n\n\ndef _build_doc_vectors_chunk(args: tuple[list[int], dict[int, dict[str, float]], dict[str, float]]) -> tuple[dict[int, dict[str, float]], dict[int, float]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build document vectors and norms for a chunk of documents.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (doc_ids, doc_term_freqs, idf)\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (doc_vectors, doc_norms)\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_ids, doc_term_freqs, idf = args\n    doc_vectors = {}\n    doc_norms = {}\n\n    for doc_id in doc_ids:\n        tf_dict = doc_term_freqs[doc_id]\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n        doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    return doc_vectors, doc_norms\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document],\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500,\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index using parallel processing.\n\n    Steps:\n    1. Tokenize all documents and compute term frequencies (parallel)\n    2. Calculate document frequencies for each term\n    3. Compute IDF scores\n    4. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores (parallel)\n    5. Compute document vector norms for cosine similarity (parallel)\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk for parallel processing\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Prepare document data for workers (avoid passing Document objects)\n    doc_data = [(doc.doc_id, doc.title, doc.content) for doc in documents]\n\n    # Step 1: Tokenize documents and compute TF (parallel)\n    chunks = [doc_data[i:i + chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(doc_data), chunk_size)]\n\n    doc_term_freqs: dict[int, dict[str, float]] = {}\n    doc_terms: dict[int, set[str]] = {}\n\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        results = list(executor.map(_process_document_chunk, chunks))\n\n    for result in results:\n        doc_term_freqs.update(result['doc_term_freqs'])\n        doc_terms.update(result['doc_terms'])\n        index.vocabulary.update(result['vocabulary'])\n\n    # Step 2: Calculate document frequencies\n    # This is done sequentially as it's relatively fast and avoids overhead\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # Step 3: Compute IDF scores\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 4: Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores (parallel)\n    vocab_list = list(index.vocabulary)\n    term_chunk_size = max(1, len(vocab_list) // num_workers)\n    term_chunks = [vocab_list[i:i + term_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(vocab_list), term_chunk_size)]\n\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        chunk_args = [(chunk, doc_term_freqs, index.idf) for chunk in term_chunks]\n        results = list(executor.map(_build_posting_list_chunk, chunk_args))\n\n    for result in results:\n        index.inverted_index.update(result)\n\n    # Step 5: Build document vectors and compute norms (parallel)\n    doc_ids = list(doc_term_freqs.keys())\n    doc_chunk_size = max(1, len(doc_ids) // num_workers)\n    doc_id_chunks = [doc_ids[i:i + doc_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(doc_ids), doc_chunk_size)]\n\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        chunk_args = [(chunk, doc_term_freqs, index.idf) for chunk in doc_id_chunks]\n        results = list(executor.map(_build_doc_vectors_chunk, chunk_args))\n\n    for doc_vectors, doc_norms in results:\n        index.doc_vectors.update(doc_vectors)\n        index.doc_norms.update(doc_norms)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Search Worker Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _search_single_query(args: tuple[str, dict, dict, dict, dict, int, dict[int, str]]) -> list[SearchResult]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for a single query.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (query, inverted_index, doc_vectors, doc_norms, idf, top_k, doc_titles)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult objects\n    \"\"\"\n    query, inverted_index, doc_vectors, doc_norms, idf, top_k, doc_titles = args\n\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in idf:\n            tfidf = tf * idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents (those containing at least one query term)\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0) for term in query_vector)\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(SearchResult(\n            doc_id=doc_id,\n            score=score,\n            title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\"),\n        ))\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef _search_query_batch(args: tuple[list[str], dict, dict, dict, dict, int, dict[int, str]]) -> list[list[SearchResult]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a batch of queries.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (queries, inverted_index, doc_vectors, doc_norms, idf, top_k, doc_titles)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of result lists, one per query\n    \"\"\"\n    queries, inverted_index, doc_vectors, doc_norms, idf, top_k, doc_titles = args\n\n    results = []\n    for query in queries:\n        query_args = (query, inverted_index, doc_vectors, doc_norms, idf, top_k, doc_titles)\n        results.append(_search_single_query(query_args))\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    documents: list[Document] = None,\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    if not queries:\n        return [], time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    # Build doc_titles lookup\n    doc_titles = {}\n    if documents:\n        doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents}\n\n    # For small number of queries, just run sequentially\n    if len(queries) < num_workers:\n        results = []\n        for query in queries:\n            query_args = (query, index.inverted_index, index.doc_vectors,\n                         index.doc_norms, index.idf, top_k, doc_titles)\n            results.append(_search_single_query(query_args))\n        elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n        return results, elapsed\n\n    # Divide queries into batches for parallel processing\n    batch_size = max(1, len(queries) // num_workers)\n    query_batches = [queries[i:i + batch_size] for i in range(0, len(queries), batch_size)]\n\n    # Prepare arguments for each batch\n    batch_args = [\n        (batch, index.inverted_index, index.doc_vectors, index.doc_norms,\n         index.idf, top_k, doc_titles)\n        for batch in query_batches\n    ]\n\n    # Execute in parallel\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        batch_results = list(executor.map(_search_query_batch, batch_args))\n\n    # Flatten results maintaining order\n    all_results = []\n    for batch_result in batch_results:\n        all_results.extend(batch_result)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n    return all_results, elapsed\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point for Testing\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Test the parallel implementation.\"\"\"\n    import argparse\n    from document_generator import generate_corpus\n    from sequential import build_tfidf_index_sequential, batch_search_sequential\n\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Parallel TF-IDF indexing and search\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-docs\", type=int, default=5000, help=\"Number of workers\")\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine Test\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Generate corpus\n    print(f\"\\nGenerating {args.num_docs} documents...\")\n    documents = generate_corpus(args.num_docs, seed=42)\n\n    # Generate test queries\n    test_queries = [\n        \"machine learning algorithm\",\n        \"distributed computing system\",\n        \"neural network training\",\n        \"data analysis pipeline\",\n        \"cloud infrastructure deployment\",\n    ] * (args.num_queries // 5)\n\n    print(f\"Number of queries: {len(test_queries)}\")\n    print(f\"Number of workers: {args.num_workers}\")\n\n    # Sequential indexing\n    print(\"\\n--- Sequential Indexing ---\")\n    seq_result = build_tfidf_index_parallel(documents, num_workers=args.num_workers)\n    print(f\"Time: {par_result.elapsed_time:.3f}s\")\n    print(f\"Speedup: {seq_result.elapsed_time / par_result.elapsed_time:.2f}x\")\n\n    # Verify correctness\n    print(\"\\n--- Verifying Index Correctness ---\")\n    assert par_result.index.num_documents == seq_result.index.num_documents\n build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document],\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500,\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index using parallel processing.\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = scores\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 4: Build inverted index (optimized sequential - uses term_to_docs for O(1) lookup)\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        posting_list = []\n        term_idf = index.idf[term]\n        for doc_id in term_to_docs[term]:\n            tf = index.inverted_index[term] = posting_list\n\n    # Step 5: Build document vectors and norms (parallel)\n    doc_ids = list(doc_term_freqs.keys())\n\n    if len(doc_ids) > 500 and num_workers > 1:\n doc_vector\n            index.doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n ============================================================================\n# Search Worker with Shared State (Global variables for worker processes)\n# doc_vector = _worker_doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = _worker_doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        dot_product = \"\"\"\n    73→    terms, term_to_docs, doc_term_freqs_idf = args\n    74→    result = {}\n    75→\n    76→    for term in terms:\n    77→        posting_list = []\n    78→        for doc_id in ============================================================================\n   110→# Parallel Index Building\n   111→# ============================================================================\n   112→\n   113→\n   114→def build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n   115→    documents: list[Document],\n   116→    num_workers: int = None,\n   117→    chunk_size: int = 500,\n   118→) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n   119→    \"\"\"\n   120→    Build TF-IDF inverted index using parallel processing.\n   121→    \"\"\"\n   122→    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n   123→\n   124→    if num_workers is None:\n   125→        num_workers = cpu_count()\n   126→\n   127→    index = TFIDFIndex()\n   128→    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n   129→\n   130→    # Prepare document data effective_chunk_size = max(chunk_size, len(doc_data) // (num_workers * 2))\n   135→    chunks = [doc_data[i:i + effective_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(doc_data), effective_chunk_size)]\n   136→\n   137→    doc_term_freqs: dict[int, dict[str, float]] = {}\n   138→    doc_terms: dict[int, set[str]] = {}\n   139→\n   140→    if len(documents) > 500 and num_workers > 1:\n   141→        with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n   142→            results = list(executor.map(_process_document_chunk, chunks))\n   143→\n   144→        for chunk_freqs, chunk_terms, else:\n   149→        for chunk in chunks:\n   150→            chunk_freqs, chunk_terms, chunk_vocab = _process_document_chunk(chunk)\n   151→            doc_term_freqs.update(chunk_freqs)\n   152→            doc_terms.update(chunk_terms)\n   153→            index.vocabulary.update(chunk_vocab)\n   154→\n   155→    # Step 2: Build term -> doc_ids mapping for faster DF computation\n   156→    term_to_docs = defaultdict(list)\n   157→    for doc_id, terms in doc_terms.items():\n   158→        for term in terms:\n   159→            term_to_docs[term].append(doc_id)\n   160→\n   161→    for for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n   167→        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n   168→\n   169→    # Step 4: Build inverted index (optimized sequential - uses term_to_docs for O(1) lookup)\n   170→    for term in index.vocabulary:\n   171→        posting_list = []\n   172→        term_idf = index.idf[term]\n   173→        for doc_id in term_to_docs[term]:\n   174→ 182→\n   183→    if len(doc_ids) > 500 and num_workers > 1:\n   184→        doc_chunk_size = max(1, len(doc_ids) // num_workers)\n   185→        doc_id_chunks = [doc_ids[i:i + doc_chunk_size] for i in f\"Document {doc_id}\"),\n   294→        ))\n   295→\n   296→    return results\n   297→\n   298→\n   299→def _search_single_query_direct(\n   300→    query: str,\n   301→    inverted_index: dict,\n len(documents)\n\n    # Prepare document data for workers\n    doc_data = [(doc.doc_id, doc.title, doc.content) for doc in documents]\n\n    # Step 1: Tokenize documents and compute TF (parallel)\n    effective_chunk_size = max(chunk_size, len(doc_data) // (num_workers * 2))\n    chunks = [doc_data[i:i + effective_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(doc_data), effective_chunk_size)]\n\n    doc_term_freqs: dict[int, dict[str, float]] = {}\n    doc_terms: dict[int, set[str]] = {}\n\n    if len(documents) > 500 and num_workers > 1:\n        with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n            results = list(executor.map(_process_document_chunk, chunks))\n\n        for chunk_freqs, chunk_terms, chunk_vocab in results:\n            doc_term_freqs.update(chunk_freqs)\n            doc_terms.update(chunk_terms)\n            index.vocabulary.update(chunk_vocab)\n    else:\n        for chunk in chunks:\n            chunk_freqs, chunk_terms, chunk_vocab = _process_document_chunk(chunk)\n            doc_term_freqs.update(chunk_freqs)\n            doc_terms.update(chunk_terms)\n            index.vocabulary.update(chunk_vocab)\n\n    # Step 2: Build term -> doc_ids mapping for faster DF computation\n    term_to_docs = defaultdict(list)\n    for doc_id, terms in doc_terms.items():\n        for term in terms:\n            term_to_docs[term].append(doc_id)\n\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = len(term_to_docs[term])\n\n    # Step 3: Compute IDF scores\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 4: Build inverted index (optimized sequential - uses term_to_docs for O(1) lookup)\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        posting_list = []\n        term_idf = index.idf[term]\n        for doc_id in term_to_docs[term]:\n            tf = doc_term_freqs[doc_id][term]\n            tfidf = tf * term_idf\n            posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        index.inverted_index[term] = posting_list\n\n    # Step 5: Build document vectors and norms (parallel)\n    doc_ids = list(doc_term_freqs.keys())\n\n    if len(doc_ids) > 500 and num_workers > 1:\n        doc_chunk_size = max(1, len(doc_ids) // num_workers)\n        doc_id_chunks = [doc_ids[i:i + doc_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(doc_ids), doc_chunk_size)]\n\n        with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n            chunk_args = [(chunk, doc_term_freqs, index.idf) for chunk in doc_id_chunks]\n            results = list(executor.map(_build_doc_vectors_chunk, chunk_args))\n\n        for doc_vectors, doc_norms in results:\n            index.doc_vectors.update(doc_vectors)\n            index.doc_norms.update(doc_norms)\n    else:\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n            doc_vector = {}\n            norm_squared = 0.0\n            for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n                norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n            index.doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n            index.doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Search Worker with Shared State (Global variables for worker processes)\n# ============================================================================\n\n# Global variables for worker processes\n_worker_inverted_index = None\n_worker_doc_vectors = None\n_worker_doc_norms = None\n_worker_idf = None\n_worker_doc_titles = None\n\n\ndef _init_search_worker(inverted_index, doc_vectors, doc_norms, idf, doc_titles):\n    \"\"\"Initialize worker with shared index data.\"\"\"\n    global _worker_inverted_index, _worker_doc_vectors, _worker_doc_norms, _worker_idf, _worker_doc_titles\n    _worker_inverted_index = inverted_index\n    _worker_doc_vectors = doc_vectors\n    _worker_doc_norms = doc_norms\n    _worker_idf = idf\n    _worker_doc_titles = doc_titles\n\n\ndef _search_query_worker(args: tuple[str, int]) -> list[SearchResult]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for a single query using worker's shared index.\n    \"\"\"\n    query, top_k = args\n\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in _worker_idf:\n            tfidf = tf * _worker_idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in _worker_inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in _worker_inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = _worker_doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = _worker_doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        dot_product = sum(query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0) for term in query_vector)\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(SearchResult(\n            doc_id=doc_id,\n            score=score,\n            title=_worker_doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\"),\n        ))\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef _search_single_query_direct(\n    query: str,\n    inverted_index: dict,\n    doc_vectors: dict,\n    doc_norms: dict,\n    idf: dict,\n    top_k: int,\n    doc_titles: dict,\n) -> list[SearchResult]:\n    \"\"\"Search for a single query directly (for small batches).\"\"\"\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in idf:\n            tfidf = tf * idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        dot_product = sum(query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0) for term in query_vector)\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(SearchResult(\n            doc_id=doc_id,\n            score=score,\n            title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\"),\n        ))\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    documents: list[Document] = None,\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel using multiprocessing.\n\n    Uses Pool with initializer to share index data across workers,\n    avoiding serialization overhead for each query.\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    if not queries:\n        return [], time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    # Build doc_titles lookup\n    doc_titles = {}\n    if documents:\n        doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents}\n\n    # For small number of queries, run sequentially to avoid overhead\n    if len(queries) < num_workers * 4:\n        results = []\n        for query in queries:\n            results.append(_search_single_query_direct(\n                query, index.inverted_index, index.doc_vectors,\n                index.doc_norms, index.idf, top_k, doc_titles\n            ))\n        elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n        return results, elapsed\n\n    # Use Pool with initializer to share index data\n    query_args = [(query, top_k) for query in queries]\n\n    with Pool(\n        processes=num_workers,\n        initializer=_init_search_worker,\n        initargs=(index.inverted_index, index.doc_vectors, index.doc_norms, index.idf, doc_titles)\n    ) as pool:\n        all_results = pool.map(_search_query_worker, query_args)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n    return all_results, elapsed\n\n\n# all_results, elapsed"," "]}],"originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nParallel TF-IDF Implementation\n\nThis module provides parallel implementations of:\n1. TF-IDF index building with multiprocessing\n2. Batch similarity search with threading (to avoid serialization overhead)\n\nAchieves speedup on multi-core systems while maintaining identical results\nto the sequential implementation.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport math\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom concurrent.futures import ProcessPoolExecutor, ThreadPoolExecutor\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom heapq import nlargest\nfrom multiprocessing import cpu_count\n\nfrom document_generator import Document\nfrom sequential import (\n    IndexingResult,\n    SearchResult,\n    TFIDFIndex,\n    compute_term_frequencies,\n    tokenize,\n)\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building Result\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Worker Functions for Index Building (Multiprocessing)\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _process_document_chunk(chunk: list[tuple[int, str, str]]) -> tuple[dict, dict, set]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a chunk of documents to compute term frequencies.\n\n    Args:\n        chunk: List of (doc_id, title, content) tuples\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary)\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n    vocabulary = set()\n\n    for doc_id, title, content in chunk:\n        text = title + \" \" + content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        doc_term_freqs[doc_id] = tf\n        doc_terms[doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        vocabulary.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document],\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500,\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index using parallel processing.\n\n    Uses multiprocessing for document tokenization (CPU-bound), then\n    sequential processing for merging and index construction.\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk for parallel processing\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Prepare document data for workers (avoid passing Document objects)\n    doc_data = [(doc.doc_id, doc.title, doc.content) for doc in documents]\n\n    # Step 1: Tokenize documents and compute TF (parallel)\n    # Use larger chunks to reduce overhead\n    effective_chunk_size = max(chunk_size, len(doc_data) // (num_workers * 2))\n    chunks = [doc_data[i:i + effective_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(doc_data), effective_chunk_size)]\n\n    doc_term_freqs: dict[int, dict[str, float]] = {}\n    doc_terms: dict[int, set[str]] = {}\n\n    # Only use multiprocessing if we have enough documents\n    if len(documents) > 1000 and num_workers > 1:\n        with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n            results = list(executor.map(_process_document_chunk, chunks))\n\n        for chunk_freqs, chunk_terms, chunk_vocab in results:\n            doc_term_freqs.update(chunk_freqs)\n            doc_terms.update(chunk_terms)\n            index.vocabulary.update(chunk_vocab)\n    else:\n        # Sequential for small datasets\n        for chunk in chunks:\n            chunk_freqs, chunk_terms, chunk_vocab = _process_document_chunk(chunk)\n            doc_term_freqs.update(chunk_freqs)\n            doc_terms.update(chunk_terms)\n            index.vocabulary.update(chunk_vocab)\n\n    # Step 2: Calculate document frequencies\n    # Build term -> doc_ids index for faster DF computation\n    term_to_docs = defaultdict(list)\n    for doc_id, terms in doc_terms.items():\n        for term in terms:\n            term_to_docs[term].append(doc_id)\n\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = len(term_to_docs[term])\n\n    # Step 3: Compute IDF scores\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 4 & 5: Build inverted index and document vectors (sequential)\n    # These steps benefit less from parallelization due to data dependencies\n    # and the overhead of transferring large dictionaries\n\n    # Build inverted index\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        posting_list = []\n        term_idf = index.idf[term]\n        for doc_id in term_to_docs[term]:\n            tf = doc_term_freqs[doc_id][term]\n            tfidf = tf * term_idf\n            posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        index.inverted_index[term] = posting_list\n\n    # Build document vectors and compute norms\n    for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n        index.doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        index.doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Search Functions (Threading - shares memory, no serialization overhead)\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _search_single_query(\n    query: str,\n    inverted_index: dict,\n    doc_vectors: dict,\n    doc_norms: dict,\n    idf: dict,\n    top_k: int,\n    doc_titles: dict,\n) -> list[SearchResult]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for a single query using the shared index.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in idf:\n            tfidf = tf * idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        dot_product = sum(query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0) for term in query_vector)\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(SearchResult(\n            doc_id=doc_id,\n            score=score,\n            title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\"),\n        ))\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    documents: list[Document] = None,\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel using threads.\n\n    Uses ThreadPoolExecutor to avoid serialization overhead - threads share\n    the same memory space so the index doesn't need to be copied.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker threads (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    if not queries:\n        return [], time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    # Build doc_titles lookup\n    doc_titles = {}\n    if documents:\n        doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents}\n\n    # Use threading to avoid serialization overhead\n    # Threads share memory, so the index is not copied\n    def search_query(query: str) -> list[SearchResult]:\n        return _search_single_query(\n            query,\n            index.inverted_index,\n            index.doc_vectors,\n            index.doc_norms,\n            index.idf,\n            top_k,\n            doc_titles,\n        )\n\n    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        all_results = list(executor.map(search_query, queries))\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n    return all_results, elapsed\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point for Testing\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Test the parallel implementation.\"\"\"\n    import argparse\n    from document_generator import generate_corpus\n    from sequential import build_tfidf_index_sequential, batch_search_sequential\n\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Parallel TF-IDF indexing and search\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-docs\", type=int, default=5000, help=\"Number of documents\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-queries\", type=int, default=100, help=\"Number of queries\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-workers\", type=int, default=4, help=\"Number of workers\")\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine Test\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Generate corpus\n    print(f\"\\nGenerating {args.num_docs} documents...\")\n    documents = generate_corpus(args.num_docs, seed=42)\n\n    # Generate test queries\n    test_queries = [\n        \"machine learning algorithm\",\n        \"distributed computing system\",\n        \"neural network training\",\n        \"data analysis pipeline\",\n        \"cloud infrastructure deployment\",\n    ] * (args.num_queries // 5)\n\n    print(f\"Number of queries: {len(test_queries)}\")\n    print(f\"Number of workers: {args.num_workers}\")\n\n    # Sequential indexing\n    print(\"\\n--- Sequential Indexing ---\")\n    seq_result = build_tfidf_index_sequential(documents)\n    print(f\"Time: {seq_result.elapsed_time:.3f}s\")\n\n    # Parallel indexing\n    print(\"\\n--- Parallel Indexing ---\")\n    par_result = build_tfidf_index_parallel(documents, num_workers=args.num_workers)\n    print(f\"Time: {par_result.elapsed_time:.3f}s\")\n    print(f\"Speedup: {seq_result.elapsed_time / par_result.elapsed_time:.2f}x\")\n\n    # Verify correctness\n    print(\"\\n--- Verifying Index Correctness ---\")\n    assert par_result.index.num_documents == seq_result.index.num_documents\n    assert par_result.index.vocabulary == seq_result.index.vocabulary\n    assert par_result.index.document_frequencies == seq_result.index.document_frequencies\n    print(\"Index verification passed!\")\n\n    # Sequential search\n    print(\"\\n--- Sequential Batch Search ---\")\n    search_start = time.perf_counter()\n    seq_search_results = batch_search_sequential(test_queries, seq_result.index, top_k=10, documents=documents)\n    seq_search_time = time.perf_counter() - search_start\n    print(f\"Time: \"\"\"\n    73→    terms, term_to_docs, doc_term_freqs_idf = args\n    74→    result = {}\n    75→\n    76→    for term in terms:\n    77→        posting_list = []\n    78→        for doc_id in ParallelIndexingResult:\n   119→    \"\"\"\n   120→    Build TF-IDF inverted index using parallel processing.\n   121→    \"\"\"\n   122→    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n   123→\n   124→    if python3\n\"\"\"\nParallel TF-IDF Implementation\n\nThis module provides parallel implementations of:\n1. TF-IDF index building with multiprocessing\n2. Batch similarity search with multiprocessing (using shared state via initializer)\n\nAchieves speedup on multi-core systems while maintaining identical results\nto the sequential implementation.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport math\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom concurrent.futures import ProcessPoolExecutor\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom heapq import nlargest\nfrom multiprocessing import Pool, cpu_count\n\nfrom document_generator import Document\nfrom sequential import (\n    SearchResult,\n    TFIDFIndex,\n    compute_term_frequencies,\n ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Worker Functions for Index Building (Multiprocessing)\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _process_document_chunk(chunk: list[tuple[int, str, str]]) -> tuple[dict, dict, set]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a chunk of documents to compute term frequencies.\n    Returns: (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary)\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n    vocabulary = set()\n\n    for doc_id, title, content in chunk:\n        text = title + \" \" + content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        doc_term_freqs[doc_id] = tf\n        doc_terms[doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        vocabulary.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary\n\n\n# Global state for index building workers\n_builder_doc_term_freqs = None\n_builder_idf = None\n_builder_term_to_docs = None\n\n\ndef _init_index_builder(doc_term_freqs, idf, term_to_docs):\n    \"\"\"Initialize index builder workers with shared data.\"\"\"\n    global _builder_doc_term_freqs, _builder_idf, _builder_term_to_docs\n    _builder_doc_term_freqs = doc_term_freqs\n    _builder_idf = idf\n    _builder_term_to_docs = term_to_docs\n\n\ndef _build_inverted_index_chunk_worker(terms: list[str]) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Build inverted index for a chunk of terms.\"\"\"\n    result = {}\n    for term in terms:\n        posting_list = []\n        term_idf = _builder_idf[term]\n        for doc_id in _builder_term_to_docs[term]:\n            tf = _builder_doc_term_freqs[doc_id][term]\n            tfidf = tf * term_idf\n            posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        result[term] = posting_list\n    return result\n\n\ndef _build_doc_vectors_chunk_worker(doc_ids: list[int]) -> tuple[dict, dict]:\n    \"\"\"Build document vectors and norms for a chunk of documents.\"\"\"\n    doc_vectors = {}\n    doc_norms = {}\n\n    for doc_id in doc_ids:\n        tf_dict = _builder_doc_term_freqs[doc_id]\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * _builder_idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n        doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    return doc_vectors, doc_norms\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document],\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500,\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index using parallel processing.\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Prepare document data for workers\n    doc_data = [(doc.doc_id, doc.title, doc.content) for doc in documents]\n\n    # Step 1: Tokenize documents and compute TF (parallel)\n    effective_chunk_size = max(chunk_size, len(doc_data) // (num_workers * 2))\n    chunks = [doc_data[i:i + effective_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(doc_data), effective_chunk_size)]\n\n    doc_term_freqs: dict[int, dict[str, float]] = {}\n    doc_terms: dict[int, set[str]] = {}\n\n    if len(documents) > 500 and num_workers > 1:\n        with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n            results = list(executor.map(_process_document_chunk, chunks))\n\n        for chunk_freqs, chunk_terms, chunk_vocab in results:\n            doc_term_freqs.update(chunk_freqs)\n            doc_terms.update(chunk_terms)\n            index.vocabulary.update(chunk_vocab)\n    else:\n        for chunk in chunks:\n            chunk_freqs, chunk_terms, chunk_vocab = _process_document_chunk(chunk)\n            doc_term_freqs.update(chunk_freqs)\n            doc_terms.update(chunk_terms)\n            index.vocabulary.update(chunk_vocab)\n\n    # Step 2: Build term -> doc_ids mapping for faster DF computation\n    term_to_docs = defaultdict(list)\n    for doc_id, terms in doc_terms.items():\n        for term in terms:\n            term_to_docs[term].append(doc_id)\n\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = len(term_to_docs[term])\n\n    # Step 3: Compute IDF scores\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Convert to regular dict for pickling\n    term_to_docs_dict = dict(term_to_docs)\n\n    # Steps 4 & 5: Build inverted index and doc vectors in parallel using Pool with initializer\n    if len(index.vocabulary) > 1000 and num_workers > 1:\n        # Prepare chunks for parallel processing\n        vocab_list = list(index.vocabulary)\n        term_chunk_size = max(1, len(vocab_list) // num_workers)\n        term_chunks = [vocab_list[i:i + term_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(vocab_list), term_chunk_size)]\n\n        doc_ids = list(doc_term_freqs.keys())\n        doc_chunk_size = max(1, len(doc_ids) // num_workers)\n        doc_id_chunks = [doc_ids[i:i + doc_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(doc_ids), doc_chunk_size)]\n\n        # Use a single Pool for both operations to amortize initialization cost\n        with Pool(\n            processes=num_workers,\n            initializer=_init_index_builder,\n            initargs=(doc_term_freqs, index.idf, term_to_docs_dict)\n        ) as pool:\n            # Build inverted index\n            inv_results = pool.map(_build_inverted_index_chunk_worker, term_chunks)\n            for result in inv_results:\n                index.inverted_index.update(result)\n\n            # Build doc vectors\n            vec_results = pool.map(_build_doc_vectors_chunk_worker, doc_id_chunks)\n            for doc_vectors, doc_norms in vec_results:\n                index.doc_vectors.update(doc_vectors)\n                index.doc_norms.update(doc_norms)\n    else:\n        # Sequential fallback for small datasets\n        for term in index.vocabulary:\n            posting_list = []\n            term_idf = index.idf[term]\n            for doc_id in term_to_docs[term]:\n                tf = doc_term_freqs[doc_id][term]\n                tfidf = tf * term_idf\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n            posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n            index.inverted_index[term] = posting_list\n\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n            doc_vector = {}\n            norm_squared = 0.0\n            for term, tf in doc_vector\n            index.doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n\n# Global variables for search worker processes\n_worker_inverted_index = None\n_worker_doc_vectors = list[SearchResult]:\n    \"\"\"Search for a single query using worker's shared index.\"\"\"\n    query, top_k = args\n\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return tfidf = tf * _worker_idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm =            candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = _worker_doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = _worker_doc_norms.get(doc_id, compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in idf:\n            tfidf = tf * idf[term]\n = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        dot_product = sum(query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0) for term in query_vector)\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(SearchResult(\n            doc_id=doc_id,\n            score=score,\n            title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\"),\n        ))\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n#    num_workers: int = None,\n    documents: list[Document] = None,\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel using multiprocessing.\n\n    Uses Pool with initializer to share index data across workers,\n    avoiding serialization overhead for each query.\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    if not queries:\n        return [], time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    # Build doc_titles lookup\n    doc_titles = {}\n    if documents:\n        doc_titles doc_titles)\n    ) as pool:\n        all_results = pool.map(_search_query_worker, query_args)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n    return all_results, elapsed\n\n\n# provides parallel implementations of:\n1. TF-IDF index building with multiprocessing\n2. Batch similarity search with multiprocessing (using shared state via initializer)\n\nAchieves speedup on multi-core systems while maintaining identical results\nto the sequential implementation.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport math\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom concurrent.futures import ProcessPoolExecutor\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom heapq import nlargest\nfrom multiprocessing import Pool, cpu_count\n\nfrom document_generator import Document\nfrom sequential import (\n    SearchResult,\n    TFIDFIndex,\n    compute_term_frequencies,\n    tokenize,\n)\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building Result\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Worker Functions for Index Building (Multiprocessing)\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _process_document_chunk(chunk: list[tuple[int, str, str]]) -> tuple[dict, dict, set]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a chunk of documents to compute term frequencies.\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n    vocabulary = set()\n\n    for doc_id, title, content in chunk:\n        text = title + \" \" + content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        doc_term_freqs[doc_id] = tf\n        doc_terms[doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        vocabulary.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary\n\n\ndef _build_inverted_index_chunk(args: tuple[list[str], dict, dict]) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build inverted index for a chunk of terms.\n    \"\"\"\n    terms, term_to_docs, doc_term_freqs_idf = args\n    result = {}\n\n    for term in terms:\n        posting_list = []\n        for doc_id in term_to_docs[term]:\n            tfidf = doc_term_freqs_idf[(doc_id, term)]\n            posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        result[term] = posting_list\n\n    return result\n\n\ndef _build_doc_vectors_chunk(args: tuple[list[int], dict, dict]) -> tuple[dict, dict]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build document vectors and norms for a chunk of documents.\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_ids, doc_term_freqs, idf = args\n    doc_vectors = {}\n    doc_norms = {}\n\n    for doc_id in doc_ids:\n        tf_dict = doc_term_freqs[doc_id]\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n        doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    return doc_vectors, doc_norms\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document],\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500,\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index using parallel processing.\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Prepare document data for workers\n    doc_data = [(doc.doc_id, doc.title, doc.content) for doc in documents]\n\n    # Step 1: Tokenize documents and compute TF (parallel)\n    effective_chunk_size = max(chunk_size, len(doc_data) // (num_workers * 2))\n    chunks = [doc_data[i:i + effective_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(doc_data), effective_chunk_size)]\n\n    doc_term_freqs: dict[int, dict[str, float]] = {}\n    doc_terms: dict[int, set[str]] = {}\n\n    if len(documents) > 500 and num_workers > 1:\n        with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n            results = list(executor.map(_process_document_chunk, chunks))\n\n        for chunk_freqs, chunk_terms, chunk_vocab in results:\n            doc_term_freqs.update(chunk_freqs)\n            doc_terms.update(chunk_terms)\n            index.vocabulary.update(chunk_vocab)\n    else:\n        for chunk in chunks:\n            chunk_freqs, chunk_terms, chunk_vocab = _process_document_chunk(chunk)\n            doc_term_freqs.update(chunk_freqs)\n            doc_terms.update(chunk_terms)\n            index.vocabulary.update(chunk_vocab)\n\n    # Step 2: Build term -> doc_ids mapping for faster DF computation\n    term_to_docs = defaultdict(list)\n    for doc_id, terms in doc_terms.items():\n        for term in terms:\n            term_to_docs[term].append(doc_id)\n\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = len(term_to_docs[term])\n\n    # Step 3: Compute IDF scores\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 4: Build inverted index (optimized sequential - uses term_to_docs for O(1) lookup)\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        posting_list = []\n        term_idf = index.idf[term]\n        for doc_id in term_to_docs[term]:\n            tf = doc_term_freqs[doc_id][term]\n            tfidf = tf * term_idf\n            posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        index.inverted_index[term] = posting_list\n\n    # Step 5: Build document vectors and norms (parallel)\n    doc_ids = list(doc_term_freqs.keys())\n\n    if len(doc_ids) > 500 and num_workers > 1:\n        doc_chunk_size = max(1, len(doc_ids) // num_workers)\n        doc_id_chunks = [doc_ids[i:i + doc_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(doc_ids), doc_chunk_size)]\n\n        with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n            chunk_args = [(chunk, doc_term_freqs, index.idf) for chunk in doc_id_chunks]\n            results = list(executor.map(_build_doc_vectors_chunk, chunk_args))\n\n        for doc_vectors, doc_norms in results:\n            index.doc_vectors.update(doc_vectors)\n            index.doc_norms.update(doc_norms)\n    else:\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n            doc_vector = {}\n            norm_squared = 0.0\n            for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n                norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n            index.doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n            index.doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Search Worker with Shared State (Global variables for worker processes)\n# ============================================================================\n\n# Global variables for worker processes\n_worker_inverted_index = None\n_worker_doc_vectors = None\n_worker_doc_norms = None\n_worker_idf = None\n_worker_doc_titles = None\n\n\ndef _init_search_worker(inverted_index, doc_vectors, doc_norms, idf, doc_titles):\n    \"\"\"Initialize worker with shared index data.\"\"\"\n    global _worker_inverted_index, _worker_doc_vectors, _worker_doc_norms, _worker_idf, _worker_doc_titles\n    _worker_inverted_index = inverted_index\n    _worker_doc_vectors = doc_vectors\n    _worker_doc_norms = doc_norms\n    _worker_idf = idf\n    _worker_doc_titles = doc_titles\n\n\ndef _search_query_worker(args: tuple[str, int]) -> list[SearchResult]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for a single query using worker's shared index.\n    \"\"\"\n    query, top_k = args\n\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in _worker_idf:\n            tfidf = tf * _worker_idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in _worker_inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in _worker_inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = _worker_doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = _worker_doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        dot_product = sum(query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0) for term in query_vector)\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(SearchResult(\n            doc_id=doc_id,\n            score=score,\n            title=_worker_doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\"),\n        ))\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef _search_single_query_direct(\n    query: str,\n    inverted_index: dict,\n    doc_vectors: dict,\n    doc_norms: dict,\n    idf: dict,\n    top_k: int,\n    doc_titles: dict,\n) -> list[SearchResult]:\n    \"\"\"Search for a single query directly (for small batches).\"\"\"\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in idf:\n            tfidf = tf * idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        dot_product = sum(query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0) for term in query_vector)\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(SearchResult(\n            doc_id=doc_id,\n            score=score,\n            title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\"),\n        ))\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    documents: list[Document] = None,\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel using multiprocessing.\n\n    Uses Pool with initializer to share index data across workers,\n    avoiding serialization overhead for each query.\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    if not queries:\n        return [], time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    # Build doc_titles lookup\n    doc_titles = {}\n    if documents:\n        doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents}\n\n    # For small number of queries, run sequentially to avoid overhead\n    if len(queries) < num_workers * 4:\n        results = []\n        for query in queries:\n            results.append(_search_single_query_direct(\n                query, index.inverted_index, index.doc_vectors,\n                index.doc_norms, index.idf, top_k, doc_titles\n            ))\n        elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n        return results, elapsed\n\n    # Use Pool with initializer to share index data\n    query_args = [(query, top_k) for query in queries]\n\n    with Pool(\n        processes=num_workers,\n        initializer=_init_search_worker,\n        initargs=(index.inverted_index, index.doc_vectors, index.doc_norms, index.idf, doc_titles)\n    ) as pool:\n        all_results = pool.map(_search_query_worker, query_args)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n    return all_results, elapsed\n\n\n# main():\n    \"\"\"Test the parallel implementation.\"\"\"\n    import argparse\n    from document_generator import generate_corpus\n    from sequential import build_tfidf_index_sequential, workers\")\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine Test\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Generate corpus\n    print(f\"\\nGenerating passed!\")\n\n    # Sequential search\n    print(\"\\n--- Sequential Batch Search ---\")\n    search_start = time.perf_counter()\n    seq_search_results = batch_search_sequential(test_queries, Search Results ---\")\n    for i, (seq_res, par_res) in enumerate(zip(seq_search_results, par_search_results)):\n        assert len(seq_res) == len(par_res), f\"Query {i}: Length mismatch\"\n
