TF-IDF index building with multiprocessing\n2. Batch similarity search with multiprocessing (using shared state via initializer)\n\nAchieves speedup on multi-core systems while maintaining identical results\nto the sequential implementation.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport math\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom concurrent.futures import ProcessPoolExecutor\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom heapq import nlargest\nfrom multiprocessing import Pool, cpu_count\n\nfrom document_generator import Document\nfrom sequential import (\n    SearchResult,\n    TFIDFIndex,\n    compute_term_frequencies,\n    tokenize,\n)\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building Result\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Worker Functions for Index Building (Multiprocessing)\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _process_document_chunk(chunk: list[tuple[int, str, str]]) -> tuple[dict, dict, set]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a chunk of documents to compute term frequencies.\n    Returns: (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary)\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n    vocabulary = set()\n\n    for doc_id, title, content in chunk:\n        text = title + \" \" + content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        doc_term_freqs[doc_id] = tf\n        doc_terms[doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        vocabulary.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary\n\n\n# Global state for index building workers\n_builder_doc_term_freqs = None\n_builder_idf = None\n_builder_term_to_docs = None\n\n\ndef _init_index_builder(doc_term_freqs, idf, term_to_docs):\n    \"\"\"Initialize index builder workers with shared data.\"\"\"\n    global _builder_doc_term_freqs, _builder_idf, _builder_term_to_docs\n    _builder_doc_term_freqs = doc_term_freqs\n    _builder_idf = idf\n    _builder_term_to_docs = term_to_docs\n\n\ndef _build_inverted_index_chunk_worker(terms: list[str]) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Build inverted index for a chunk of terms.\"\"\"\n    result = {}\n    for term in terms:\n        posting_list = []\n        term_idf = _builder_idf[term]\n        for doc_id in _builder_term_to_docs[term]:\n            tf = _builder_doc_term_freqs[doc_id][term]\n            tfidf = tf * term_idf\n            posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        result[term] = posting_list\n    return result\n\n\ndef _build_doc_vectors_chunk_worker(doc_ids: list[int]) -> tuple[dict, dict]:\n    \"\"\"Build document vectors and norms for a chunk of documents.\"\"\"\n    doc_vectors = {}\n    doc_norms = {}\n\n    for doc_id in doc_ids:\n        tf_dict = _builder_doc_term_freqs[doc_id]\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * _builder_idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n        doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    return doc_vectors, doc_norms\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document],\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500,\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index using parallel processing.\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Prepare document data for workers\n    doc_data = [(doc.doc_id, doc.title, doc.content) for doc in documents]\n\n    # Step 1: Tokenize documents and compute TF (parallel)\n    effective_chunk_size = max(chunk_size, len(doc_data) // (num_workers * 2))\n    chunks = [doc_data[i:i + effective_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(doc_data), effective_chunk_size)]\n\n    doc_term_freqs: dict[int, dict[str, float]] = {}\n    doc_terms: dict[int, set[str]] = {}\n\n    if len(documents) > 500 and num_workers > 1:\n        with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n            results = list(executor.map(_process_document_chunk, chunks))\n\n        for chunk_freqs, chunk_terms, chunk_vocab in results:\n            doc_term_freqs.update(chunk_freqs)\n            doc_terms.update(chunk_terms)\n            index.vocabulary.update(chunk_vocab)\n    else:\n        for chunk in chunks:\n            chunk_freqs, chunk_terms, chunk_vocab = _process_document_chunk(chunk)\n            doc_term_freqs.update(chunk_freqs)\n            doc_terms.update(chunk_terms)\n            index.vocabulary.update(chunk_vocab)\n\n    # Step 2: Build term -> doc_ids mapping for faster DF computation\n    term_to_docs = defaultdict(list)\n    for doc_id, terms in doc_terms.items():\n        for term in terms:\n            term_to_docs[term].append(doc_id)\n\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = len(term_to_docs[term])\n\n    # Step 3: Compute IDF scores\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Convert to regular dict for pickling\n    term_to_docs_dict = dict(term_to_docs)\n\n    # Steps 4 & 5: Build inverted index and doc vectors in parallel using Pool with initializer\n    if len(index.vocabulary) > 1000 and num_workers > 1:\n        # Prepare chunks for parallel processing\n        vocab_list = list(index.vocabulary)\n        term_chunk_size = max(1, len(vocab_list) // num_workers)\n        term_chunks = [vocab_list[i:i + term_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(vocab_list), term_chunk_size)]\n\n        doc_ids = list(doc_term_freqs.keys())\n        doc_chunk_size = max(1, len(doc_ids) // num_workers)\n        doc_id_chunks = [doc_ids[i:i + doc_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(doc_ids), doc_chunk_size)]\n\n        # Use a single Pool for both operations to amortize initialization cost\n        with Pool(\n            processes=num_workers,\n            initializer=_init_index_builder,\n            initargs=(doc_term_freqs, index.idf, term_to_docs_dict)\n        ) as pool:\n            # Build inverted index\n            inv_results = pool.map(_build_inverted_index_chunk_worker, term_chunks)\n            for result in inv_results:\n                index.inverted_index.update(result)\n\n            # Build doc vectors\n            vec_results = pool.map(_build_doc_vectors_chunk_worker, doc_id_chunks)\n            for doc_vectors, doc_norms in vec_results:\n                index.doc_vectors.update(doc_vectors)\n                index.doc_norms.update(doc_norms)\n    else:\n        # Sequential fallback for small datasets\n        for term in index.vocabulary:\n            posting_list = []\n            term_idf = index.idf[term]\n            for doc_id in term_to_docs[term]:\n                tf = doc_term_freqs[doc_id][term]\n                tfidf = tf * term_idf\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n            posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n            index.inverted_index[term] = posting_list\n\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n            doc_vector = {}\n            norm_squared = 0.0\n            for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n                norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n            index.doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n            index.doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Search Worker with Shared State (Global variables for worker processes)\n# ============================================================================\n\n# Global variables for search worker processes\n_worker_inverted_index = None\n_worker_doc_vectors = None\n_worker_doc_norms = None\n_worker_idf = None\n_worker_doc_titles = None\n\n\ndef _init_search_worker(inverted_index, doc_vectors, doc_norms, idf, doc_titles):\n    \"\"\"Initialize worker with shared index data.\"\"\"\n    global _worker_inverted_index, _worker_doc_vectors, _worker_doc_norms, _worker_idf, _worker_doc_titles\n    _worker_inverted_index = inverted_index\n    _worker_doc_vectors = doc_vectors\n    _worker_doc_norms = doc_norms\n    _worker_idf = idf\n    _worker_doc_titles = doc_titles\n\n\ndef _search_query_worker(args: tuple[str, int]) -> list[SearchResult]:\n    \"\"\"Search for a single query using worker's shared index.\"\"\"\n    query, top_k = args\n\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in _worker_idf:\n            tfidf = tf * _worker_idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in _worker_inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in _worker_inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = _worker_doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = _worker_doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        dot_product = sum(query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0) for term in query_vector)\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(SearchResult(\n            doc_id=doc_id,\n            score=score,\n            title=_worker_doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\"),\n        ))\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef _search_single_query_direct(\n    query: str,\n    inverted_index: dict,\n    doc_vectors: dict,\n    doc_norms: dict,\n    idf: dict,\n    top_k: int,\n    doc_titles: dict,\n) -> list[SearchResult]:\n    \"\"\"Search for a single query directly (for small batches).\"\"\"\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in idf:\n            tfidf = tf * idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        dot_product = sum(query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0) for term in query_vector)\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(SearchResult(\n            doc_id=doc_id,\n            score=score,\n            title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\"),\n        ))\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    documents: list[Document] = None,\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel using multiprocessing.\n\n    Uses Pool with initializer to share index data across workers,\n    avoiding serialization overhead for each query.\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    if not queries:\n        return [], time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    # Build doc_titles lookup\n    doc_titles = {}\n    if documents:\n        doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents}\n\n    # For small number of queries, run sequentially to avoid overhead\n    if len(queries) < num_workers * 4:\n        results = []\n        for query in queries:\n            results.append(_search_single_query_direct(\n                query, index.inverted_index, index.doc_vectors,\n                index.doc_norms, index.idf, top_k, doc_titles\n            ))\n        elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n        return results, elapsed\n\n    # Use Pool with initializer to share index data\n    query_args = [(query, top_k) for query in queries]\n\n    with Pool(\n        processes=num_workers,\n        initializer=_init_search_worker,\n        initargs=(index.inverted_index, index.doc_vectors, index.doc_norms, index.idf, doc_titles)\n    ) as pool:\n        all_results = pool.map(_search_query_worker, query_args)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n    return all_results, elapsed\n\n\n#
